<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#214b3a" />
    <meta name="description" content="Discover BSGM School, Bhagyalaxmi Sarla Gurusahani Memorial. English Medium, Co-Educational, Maharashtra State Board. Explore academics, campus life and admissions for 2026-2027." />
    <meta property="og:title" content="BSGM School | Meaningful Education. Values for Life." />
    <meta property="og:description" content="Quality holistic education. Preparing children for life. Admissions open for 2026-2027." />
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta property="og:site_name" content="BSGM School" />
    <meta property="og:image" content="https://www.bsgmschool.com/assets/2.jpg" />
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
    <link rel="icon" type="image/jpeg" href="https://www.bsgmschool.com/assets/bsgm-school-logo.jpg" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://www.bsgmschool.com" />
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@400;450;500;550;600;650;700&family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;650;700;750;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
    <link rel="preload" as="image" href="https://www.bsgmschool.com/assets/2.jpg" fetchpriority="high" />
    <title>BSGM School | Meaningful Education. Values for Life.</title>
    <script type="application/ld+json">
      {
        "@context": "https://schema.org",
        "@type": "School",
        "name": "Bhagyalaxmi Sarla Gurusahani Memorial (BSGM)",
        "alternateName": "BSGM School",
        "foundingDate": "2003",
        "logo": "https://www.bsgmschool.com/assets/bsgm-school-logo.jpg",
        "image": "https://www.bsgmschool.com/assets/bsgm-introduction.jpg",
        "telephone": "02402991488",
        "email": "bsgmschool@gmail.com",
        "parentOrganization": {
          "@type": "Organization",
          "name": "Dr. Gurusahani Foundation Trust"
        },
        "address": {
          "@type": "PostalAddress",
          "streetAddress": "Gurusahani Nagar, N-4, CIDCO",
          "addressLocality": "Aurangabad",
          "addressRegion": "Maharashtra",
          "postalCode": "431005"
        }
      }
    </script>

Day",category:"Sports"}],bs=[{title:"Classrooms",image:"classrooms.jpg",description:"Light-filled spaces for meaningful learning.",href:"#/facilities/classrooms"},{title:"Science Lab",image:"science-lab.jpg",description:"Hands-on discovery. A spirit of enquiry.",href:"#/facilities/science"},{title:"Computer Lab",image:"computer-lab.jpg",description:"Practical skills for a changing world.",href:"#/facilities/computer"},{title:"Performance Stage",image:"performance-stage.jpg",description:"A space for expression and creativity.",href:"#/facilities/stage"},{title:"Bus Transportation",image:"bus-service.jpg",description:"School transport serving Aurangabad.",href:"#/facilities/transport"}],jg=["Bhagyalaxmi Sarla Gurusahani Memorial (BSGM) started under the aegis of Dr. Gurusahani Foundation Trust in 2001, with the primary objective of providing quality holistic education at an affordable cost and preparing children for life. The school started in 2003 and has since provided meaningful education to children who aspire to grow into educated and liberated human beings.","Within a caring and healthy atmosphere, the school fosters confidence, discipline, clarity in thought and decision-making ability, the capacity to set and achieve goals, and social responsibility as a lifelong process.","The school is managed by the Trustees of Gurusahani Foundation, with qualified and dedicated staff, well-equipped laboratories, a rich library with constantly enriching additions, modern educational technology, playgrounds covering more than 2 acres, games and sports facilities, and a modern computer lab with audio-visual learning facilities.","Besides academic excellence, the school is committed to inculcating strong ethical values of integrity, politeness, kindness and respect for elders. It is a permanently non-granted English Medium, Co-Educational school affiliated to the Maharashtra State Board of Secondary and Higher Secondary Education.","BSGM is committed to enhancing the intellectual growth of students. In addition to the prescribed syllabus, further approved learning is provided for their all-round development. The original school website states that BSGM is ISO 9001:2015 accredited."],Mn={"/about":{title:"Preparing children for life.",category:"About BSGM",group:"About Us",intro:"A caring environment. Meaningful education. Values that last a lifetime.",image:"bsgm-introduction.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM School campus photograph",source:"index.html",sections:[{title:"Introduction",paragraphs:jg}]},"/about/inspiration":{title:"Our Inspiration",category:"About BSGM",group:"About Us",image:"bhagyalaxmi-sarla.jpg",imageAlt:"The original Bhagyalaxmi Sarla memorial photograph",portrait:!0,source:"our-inspiration-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Dr. Chimandas Gursahani",paragraphs:["The great visionary, to whose inspiration and commitment the inception of this school is due. His extraordinary leadership qualities, dynamism and devotion to excellence continue to guide and inspire us."]},{title:"Patron's Vision",bullets:["To provide apex all-round education in English Medium to the intermediate block of society within an affordable cost.","To foster all-round development of children through extracurricular activities in an exciting, challenging and innovative environment.","To prepare them for success in today's highly competitive world and develop a mindset needed to be responsible citizens."]}]},"/about/philosophy":{title:"Our Philosophy",category:"About BSGM",group:"About Us",source:"philosophy-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Vision",paragraphs:["The BSGM School aims to be an institution of excellence, dedicated to producing leaders of the future."]},{title:"Objective",bullets:["To provide quality holistic education and prepare children for life.","To make learning a pleasurable and enriching experience for students.","To help children become open-minded, autonomous and lifelong learners.","To promote among children a rational, pluralistic and democratic outlook on life.","To inculcate basic universal values like honesty, simplicity, hard work and a taste for excellence."]}]},"/committee/chairman":{title:"Chairman's Desk",category:"Managing Committee",group:"Managing Committee",intro:"Dr. Hiroo Gursahani, M.Sc., Ph.D. | Chairman, Dr. Gurusahani Foundation",image:"img/ChairmanSirM.JPG",imageAlt:"Dr. Hiroo Gursahani, Chairman",portrait:!0,source:"managing-committee-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"A message from our Chairman",paragraphs:["From little acorns grow mighty oaks! It fills my heart and soul with happiness and pride to see the fabulous growth of our Bhagyalaxmi Sarla Gurusahani Memorial School. I have a deep sense of satisfaction for having contributed my mite to the cause of education, in the memory of my beloved mother.","With more and more parents admitting their wards in our school, I am sure they must be having dreams for the future and expecting Bhagyalaxmi Sarla Memorial School to achieve their goals. I assure you, we will do our best to satisfy their expectations.","The school endeavours to provide an environment that encourages growth, fosters creativity, exploration and discovery, and supports all-round development through discovering the joy of learning, illuminating the intellect in multi-dimensional ways, and instilling abiding values.","Our children are the future of our nation. We want them to excel as students, make a success of their careers, be responsible citizens and, above all, be good human beings.","I congratulate and express my thanks to all the contributors for having done a wonderful job in the development of the institute."]}]},"/committee/trustee":{title:"Managing Trustee",category:"Managing Committee",group:"Managing Committee",intro:"Dr. Yash Hiroo Gursahani, M.Sc. (Bio-Technology) | Managing Trustee, Dr. Gurusahani Foundation",image:"img/ManagingTrustee.JPG",imageAlt:"Dr. Yash Hiroo Gursahani, Managing Trustee",portrait:!0,source:"managing-committee2-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Dear Parents,",paragraphs:["For us at BSGM School, molding young minds and stretching them to extents never imagined is paramount. We believe in maintaining a carefully planned and balanced curriculum. This mix of academics, sports, performing arts and social welfare initiatives makes our school a holistic learning experience for your child.","Serving the intermediate block of society, we have pledged to give the world to our students at a moderate cost. Preparing children for life calls for equipping them with finer values and academic competencies.","We have designed a curriculum which brings out the hidden potential of every child. Apart from regular classroom teaching, we ensure that students are exposed to hands-on training on the aspects they have learnt in class. Concepts are also made clearer with video lessons, emphasizing learning, writing and seeing.","Our qualified, committed and caring staff works dedicatedly to bring the best out of children and make them responsible, well-rounded individuals that define the future."]}]},"/committee/team":{title:"Our Team",category:"Managing Committee",group:"Managing Committee",source:"core-team-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Dedicated to meaningful learning",paragraphs:["The faculty at BSGM School is trained, qualified and experienced, with a track record of delivering the various curricula the school offers.","Selected by a rigorous process of test demonstrations and personal interviews, our teachers are innovative and creative in their delivery while empathizing and synergizing with children. They are excited about learning and are strong communicators with good subject knowledge and creativity.","With a generosity of mind and spirit, our teachers are open and flexible to change. The management provides trust and support in innovating their techniques and methodologies."]}]},"/academics/programme":{title:"Academic Programme",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"academic-programme-bsgm.html",image:"classrooms.jpg",imageAlt:"Students in an original BSGM classroom photograph",sections:[{title:"A balanced approach to learning",paragraphs:["We adopt a teaching methodology that mixes traditional and play-way methods of teaching and learning, and see to it that no child is left behind.","We emphasize multiple-language learning by teaching Hindi, Marathi, Sanskrit and German apart from English."]},{title:"Learning beyond the classroom",bullets:["Physical activity through Football, Cricket, Basketball, Swimming, Kho-kho, Table Tennis, Table Soccer, Skating, Karate and Chess.","A rich collection of multilingual books and learning resources in the school library.","Teacher-training workshops to update content and teaching-learning skills.","Well-equipped laboratories supporting experimental and practical learning.","A school bus transport system designed with the safety of children in mind."]}]},"/academics/examinations":{title:"Examination Procedure",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"examination-procedure-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Continuous and comprehensive evaluation",paragraphs:["The original school website describes Continuous and Comprehensive Evaluation (CCE) for students up to Class X. Its aim is to shift examination-centered learning towards holistic education, evaluating learners throughout the year according to their abilities.","Evaluation should be a positive input for improving teaching and learning. The report card reflects scholastic aspects, life skills, personality attributes, behaviour, interests, attitudes, values and proficiency in co-curricular activities."]},{title:"Examination specifications",bullets:["The academic session begins in June and the curriculum is divided into two terms.","Each term includes two unit tests and a term examination.","Unit tests are described in the source as taking place in August, September, January and February.","The first term examination is completed by October. Annual examinations take place between March and April.","The published annual-report weightage is 50% Half-Yearly Examination and 50% Annual Examination.","The final report card shows grades."]}]},"/academics/discipline":{title:"Rules of Discipline",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"rules-of-discipline-bsgm.html",intro:"A safe, stimulating school environment with a positive approach to learning and development.",sections:[{title:"Attendance and presentation",bullets:["Students are expected to attend school regularly and punctually.","Students may enter the school only in the prescribed school uniform.","Students should come neatly dressed in properly ironed clothes and polished shoes. The published rules state that untidy students may be liable to disciplinary action.","Latecomers may be sent home or fined.","A written application must be submitted to the class teacher in case of absenteeism."]},{title:"Respect and responsibility",bullets:["Students are expected to conduct themselves in a dignified and responsible manner.","All students should be polite, courteous and respectful, and greet their teachers.","Silence and discipline must be maintained in the classroom.","Damage to school property may lead to a fine in accordance with the damage caused."]},{title:"Personal belongings",bullets:["The school is not responsible for loss of articles of value. Expensive watches and jewellery are prohibited.","Mobile phones and other electronic gadgets are not permitted on the premises. The published rules allow management to confiscate them if found."]}]},"/academics/curriculum":{title:"Our Curriculum",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"curriculum-bsgm.html",intro:"Carefully planned learning, from Standard I to X.",sections:[{title:"Learning and discovery",paragraphs:["The academic syllabus is planned to help children enjoy learning and discovery, and acquire a sound understanding of mathematics, science, languages, social studies, environmental education, arts and physical education.","The school is affiliated to the Maharashtra State Board of Secondary and Higher Secondary Education and has English as the medium of instruction. The school year has two terms: June to October and November to April."]},{title:"Primary: Classes I-IV",bullets:["English, Hindi and Marathi","Mathematics, Social Studies and Science","Computer Science","Arts: Drawing, Painting and Crafts","Music and Physical Training","Work Experience for Classes III and IV"]},{title:"Middle School",bullets:["English, Hindi and Marathi","German for Class VII","Mathematics, Science and Social Studies","Art and Work Experience","Music: Indian and Western"]}]},"/academics/uniform":{title:"School Uniform",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"school-uniform-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"The prescribed school uniform",paragraphs:["All students of BSGM School should wear the prescribed uniform. Taking student comfort into consideration, superior-quality cotton fabric in soothing colours has been used.","Uniform details and the system for procurement are provided upon admission to the school."]}]},"/academics/leave":{title:"Leave / Absents",category:"Academics",group:"Academics",source:"leave-absents-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Leave applications",paragraphs:["No leave or absence is granted without a prior application from parents or guardians. A medical certificate is required with a leave application.","Vacation dates must be strictly followed and students must return on the specified date. The original published policy states a fine of Rs. 50 per day for absence without prior permission or a valid health certificate."]},{title:"Regular attendance",paragraphs:["Promotion to the next class can be withheld because of irregular attendance.","The first and last day of each term are important and must not be missed. The source states a fine of Rs. 50 for missing these days without prior permission. Please confirm the current policy with the school office."]}]},"/life/sports":{title:"Sports",category:"Life at BSGM",group:"Life at BSGM",image:"6.jpg",imageAlt:"Sport at BSGM School",source:"life-at-bsgm-sports.html",sections:[{title:"Space to play. Room to grow.",paragraphs:["The original school website highlights its sports faculty and opportunities for physical activity. Playgrounds cover more than 2 acres, with games and sports facilities for students."],bullets:["Football, Cricket and Basketball","Swimming, Kho-kho and Skating","Table Tennis and Table Soccer","Karate and Chess"]}]},"/life/parent-teacher-meeting":{title:"Parent-Teacher Meeting",category:"Life at BSGM",group:"Life at BSGM",sections:[],placeholder:"Parent-teacher meeting dates and detailed arrangements are not currently available here. Please contact the school office for the latest information."},"/life/performing-arts":{title:"Performing Arts",category:"Life at BSGM",group:"Life at BSGM",image:"7.png",imageAlt:"The original BSGM Annual Day photograph",source:"performance-stage.html",sections:[{title:"A stage for self-expression",paragraphs:["Creative art and performances are an integral part of BSGM School. The performance stage is used by students to showcase talent and learn to respect an audience.","The original events page describes class programmes involving drama, dance and music."]}]},"/life/creativity":{title:"Creativity",category:"Life at BSGM",group:"Life at BSGM",source:"life-at-bsgm-creativity.html",sections:[{title:"Encouraging every unique talent",paragraphs:["At BSGM, each child is seen as having a unique talent. The school endeavours to encourage and inspire budding artists, with art as an essential discipline.","Opportunities include clay modelling, painting, drawing and craft. Clay modelling supports self-expression, tactile awareness, design and art appreciation."]},{title:"Creative interests",bullets:["Painting and Warli Painting","Pottery and Crafts","Gardening","Plumbing and Carpentry","Candle Making and Weaving"]}]},"/life/houses":{title:"Houses",category:"Life at BSGM",group:"Life at BSGM",image:"examination-procedure-competition.jpg",imageAlt:"An original BSGM house competition photograph",source:"life-at-bsgm-houses.html",sections:[{title:"Team spirit and healthy competition",paragraphs:["The school follows a traditional House System with Red, Yellow, Green and Blue Houses. Houses provide a platform for students to exhibit their skills through inter-house activities and compete for the Best House Trophy.","Activities include debates, quizzes, painting competitions and sports. The student council is formed each year during the Investiture Ceremony."]}]},"/horizons/community":{title:"Community Services",category:"Wider Horizons",group:"Wider Horizons",sections:[],placeholder:"Community service programme details are not currently available here. Please contact the school for current information."},"/horizons/trips":{title:"Trips & Tours",category:"Wider Horizons",group:"Wider Horizons",image:"4.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM camp photograph",source:"trips-tours.html",sections:[{title:"The world beyond the classroom",paragraphs:["Students are encouraged to widen their horizons through activities and outings that vary in purpose and length. Tours help students seek information about history, geography and science, and visit local institutions and places of interest.","Educational tours typically take place at least once in an academic year. Venues are chosen with learning objectives in mind, and students and parents are prepared with a lecture highlighting the important features of the venue.","During visits, students document their impressions. At the end, they are assessed through presentations of what they have learnt, making each educational tour an in-depth learning experience."]}]},"/horizons/celebrations":{title:"Events & Celebrations",category:"Wider Horizons",group:"Wider Horizons",image:"2.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM 15th August celebration photograph",source:"event-celebration.html",sections:[{title:"Learning through shared experiences",paragraphs:["School events support classroom activities and offer new opportunities for learning. They encourage appreciation of our country's rich heritage and diversity, national pride and respect for all.","Throughout the year, classes present stage programmes based on festivals or thought-provoking ideas that lend themselves to drama, dance or music."],bullets:["Grandparents' Day, Teachers' Day and Children's Day","Independence Day and Republic Day","Diwali, Janmashtami, Christmas and Makar Sankranti","Marathi Day, English Day and Sanskrit Day"]}]},"/admissions":{title:"A new chapter begins here.",category:"Admissions 2026-2027",group:"Admission",source:"admission-bsgm.html",intro:"Admissions Open for the year 2026-2027",sections:[{title:"Admission Procedure",paragraphs:["BSGM School is primarily meant to cater to the educational aspirations of students from the intermediate class of society.","No discrimination is made on the grounds of caste, creed, race, status or gender. The school adheres to the guidelines of the Maharashtra State Board of Secondary and Higher Secondary Education and the Directorate of Education.","The admission process is transparent and carried out in accordance with laid-down policies and instructions issued from time to time."]},{title:"Verification and confirmation",paragraphs:["All admissions are provisional and are confirmed only after verification of the necessary documents. Incorrect information on the application form results in disqualification.","The school does not accept donations or capitation fees. The Admission Committee reserves its rights on issues regarding admission."]}]},"/admissions/fees":{title:"Fee Structure",category:"Admissions",group:"Admission",source:"fees-bsgm.html",sections:[{title:"Quality education at an affordable cost",paragraphs:["The original school website describes the fee structure as reasonable for the intermediate class of society, without compromising on the quality of education."]}],placeholder:"The detailed fee schedule is not currently available here. Please contact the school office directly for the verified 2026-2027 fee structure."},"/campus":{title:"Our Campus",category:"Campus & Location",group:"Campus",source:"campus-bsgm.html",image:"bsgm-school.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM School building photograph",sections:[{title:"Location",paragraphs:["The BSGM School is strategically located in the heart of the city. This prime location offers connectivity with easy access to leading terminals and areas of the city.",se.address]}]},"/facilities/classrooms":{title:"Classrooms",category:"Our Facilities",group:"Facilities",image:"classrooms.jpg",imageAlt:"Original BSGM classroom photograph",source:"classrooms.html",sections:[{title:"The right environment to learn",paragraphs:["Classrooms are large and airy, with abundant natural light to provide the right ambience for an effective learning experience. A noticeboard is decorated by teachers and students for different events throughout the academic year."]}]},"/facilities/science":{title:"Science Laboratory",category:"Our Facilities",group:"Facilities",image:"science-lab.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM science laboratory photograph",source:"science-lab.html",sections:[{title:"A spirit of questioning and enquiry",paragraphs:["The school has well-equipped and spacious laboratories for students. The science lab allows young scientists to foster a spirit of questioning and enquiry.","Students get hands-on learning experiences, with experts helping them understand how the natural world functions and observe cause-and-effect relationships."]}]},"/facilities/computer":{title:"Computer Laboratory",category:"Our Facilities",group:"Facilities",image:"computer-lab.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM computer laboratory photograph",source:"computer-lab.html",sections:[{title:"Practical skills for everyday life",paragraphs:["The school recognizes the key role of computers in today's world. Computers help students develop a mathematical and scientific approach towards problem solving.","The school emphasizes computing skills and making computers useful in day-to-day activity. A well-equipped and up-to-date computer lab gives students practical skills through individual use of the machines."]}]},"/facilities/stage":{title:"Performance Stage",category:"Our Facilities",group:"Facilities",image:"performance-stage.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM performance stage photograph",source:"performance-stage.html",sections:[{title:"Where talent finds its voice",paragraphs:["Creative art and performances are an integral part of BSGM School. The performance stage is used by students to showcase talent and learn to respect an audience."]}]},"/facilities/transport":{title:"Bus Transportation",category:"Our Facilities",group:"Facilities",image:"bus-service.jpg",imageAlt:"The original BSGM school transportation photograph",source:"bus-transportation.html",sections:[{title:"School transportation",paragraphs:["The original transportation page describes a fleet of 2 buses covering most parts of Aurangabad.
